raise NotImplementedError\n\n\nclass CompositeObserver(PipelineObserver):\n    \"\"\"\n    Allows multiple observers to be treated as a single entity.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    __slots__ = ----------------------------------------------------------------\n    def attach(self, observer: PipelineObserver) -> None:\n        self._observers.append(observer)\n\n    def detach(self, observer: PipelineObserver) -> None:\n        self._observers.remove(observer)\n\n    # PipelineObserver ----------------------------------------------------------\n    def update(self, event: PipelineEvent) -> None:\n        for obs in list(self._observers):  # shallow copy to support detach inside\n            try:\n                obs.update(event)\n            except Exception:  # pragma: no cover\n                LOGGER.exception(\"Uncaught error in observer %s\", obs.__class__.__name__)\n\n\nclass LoggingObserver(PipelineObserver):\n    \"\"\"\n    Basic observer that logs every pipeline event.\n self._SparkSession.builder.appName(f\"Backfill-{ctx.job_id}\").getOrCreate()\n        try:\n            df = spark.read.json(ctx.config.source_uri)\n            rows = df.count()\n --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\ndef _run_expectation_suite(ctx: BackfillContext) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Execute Great Expectations validation if Available: {available}\") from exc\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Scheduling                                                                  #\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\nclass BackfillScheduler:\n    \"\"\"\n    Cron-like scheduler responsible for enqueueing back-fill jobs based on\n    BackfillJobConfig definitions.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, runner: Optional[BackfillRunner] = None):\n        self._runner = runner or BackfillRunner()\n        self._scheduler = (\n            BackgroundScheduler(daemon=True) if BackgroundScheduler else None\n        )\n        self._jobs: Dict[str, BackfillJobConfig] = {}\n\n        if self._scheduler:\n            self._scheduler.start()\n            LOGGER.info(\"APScheduler started in background mode.\")\n        else:\n            LOGGER.warning(\"APScheduler not available; falling back to polling loop.\")\n\n        # Internal helpers ----------------------------------------------------------\n    def _poll_loop(self) -> None:  # pragma: no cover\n        LOGGER.debug(\"Entering naïve polling loop.\")\n        while not self._stop_event.is_set():\n            current_minute = _dt.datetime.utcnow().replace(second=0, microsecond=0)\n            for cfg in self._jobs.values():\n                if self._cron_matches(current_minute, cfg.schedule_cron):\n                    LOGGER.debug(\"Triggering job %s via naïve scheduler.\", cfg.job_name)\n                    threading.Thread(\n                        target=self._runner.run, args=(cfg,), daemon=True\n                    ).start()\n            time.sleep(60)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _cron_matches(moment: _dt.datetime, cron_expr: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"\n        Very naïve cron matcher that supports only 'minute hour * * *'.\n        \"\"\"\n        try:\n            minute, hour, *_ = cron_expr.split()\n            return (\n                (minute == \"*\" or int(minute) == moment.minute)\n                and (hour == \"*\" or int(hour) == moment.hour)\n            )\n        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover\n            LOGGER.error(\"Invalid cron expression: %s\", cron_expr)\n            return False\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _parse_cron(cron_expr: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Convert POSIX cron string to APScheduler kwargs.\n        Supports 'minute hour day month day_of_week'.\n        \"\"\"\n        minute, hour, day, month, dow = cron_expr.split()\n        return {\n            \"minute\": minute,\n            \"hour\": hour,\n            \"day\": day,\n            \"month\": month,\n            \"day_of_week\": dow,\n        }\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# CLI entry-point                                                             #\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\ndef _example_cli() -> None:  # \"action_list_operator\",\n            assets_to_validate=[(batch_kwargs, suite)],\n        )\n\n        return results[\"success\"]  # type: ignore[index]\n\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- #\nclass SentimentTransformer(BaseTransformer):\n    \"\"\"Extremely naive sentiment estimator (placeholder for ML model).\"\"\"\n\n    async def transform(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        text: str = data.get(\"payload\", {}).get(\"text\", \"\")\n        score = (sum(ord(c) for c in text) % 100) / 100  # Fake score in [0, 1]\n        label: str\n        if score > 0.6:\n            label = \"positive\"\n        elif score < 0.4:\n            label = \"negative\"\n        else:\n            label = data.setdefault(\"enrichments\", {})[\"toxicity\"] = {\"score\": score, \"flagged\": score > 0.8}\n        return data\n\n\n# Virality + metrics.get(\"retweets\", 0) * 0.5\n            + metrics.get(\"replies\", 0) * 0.1\n        ) / 1_000.0\n        data.setdefault(\"enrichments\", {})[\"virality\"] = {\"score\": min(1.0, score)}\n \"\"\"Factory that converts CLI strings to transformer instances.\"\"\"\n    transformers: List[BaseTransformer] = []\n\n    for name in names:\n        try:\n            strat = Lint few fields; for brevity we validate a subset.\n        suite.add_expectation(\n            {\n                \"expectation_type\": \"expect_column_values_to_not_be_null\",\n                \"kwargs\": {\"column\": \"event_id\"},\n            }\n        )\n        suite.add_expectation(\n            {\n                \"expectation_type\": \"expect_column_min_to_be_between\",\n                \"kwargs\": {\n                    \"column\": \"sentiment\",\n                    \"min_value\": -1.0,\n                    \"max_value\": -1.0,\n                    \"mostly\": 0.05,  # allow 95 % missing\n                },\n            }\n        )\n        suite.add_expectation(\n            {\n                \"expectation_type\": \"expect_column_values_to_be_between\",\n                \"kwargs\": {\n                    \"column\": \"toxicity\",\n                    \"min_value\": 0.0,\n                    \"max_value\": 1.0,\n                    \"ignore_row_if\": \"all_values_are_missing\",\n                },\n            }\n        )\n        return suite\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    @property\n    def name(self) -> str:  # noqa: D401 – concise property doc\n        return \"great_expectations\"\n\n    def validate(self, record: EventRecord) -> ValidationResult:  # noqa: D401\n        start = time.perf_counter()\n\n        # Coerce record to Pandas row for GE.\n        import pandas as pd\n\n        df = pd.DataFrame([record.model_dump()])\n        from great_expectations.core.batch import BatchRequest\n        from great_expectations.validator.validator import Validator\n        from great_expectations.data_context import get_context\n\n        context = get_context()\n        batch_request = BatchRequest(\n            datasource_name=\"pandas_datasource\",\n            data_connector_name=\"default_runtime_data_connector_name\",\n            data_asset_name=\"event_dataframe\",\n            runtime_parameters={\"batch_data\": df},\n            batch_identifiers={\"default_identifier_name\": \"event_validation\"},\n        )\n\n        validator: Validator = context.get_validator(\n            batch_request=batch_request, expectation_suite=self._expectation_suite\n        )\n        res = validator.validate()\n\n        duration_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000)\n        return ValidationResult(\n            strategy_name=self.name,\n            ok=res.success,\n            errors=[str(d) for d in res.results if not d.success],\n            duration_ms=duration_ms,\n        )\n\n\n# -- Strategy 2: Domain-specific rulebook ---------------------------------- #\n\n\nclass ToxicityBoundsStrategy(BaseValidationStrategy):\n    \"\"\"\n    Lightweight built-in strategy verifying toxicity <= policy threshold.\n\n    Allows rapid **fail-fast** without incurring GE overhead.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, max_toxicity: float = 0.92) -> None:\n        if not 0 <= max_toxicity <= 1:\n            raise ValueError(\"max_toxicity must be within [0, 1]\")\n        self._max_toxicity = max_toxicity\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    @property\n    def name(self) -> str:\n        return \"toxicity_bounds\"\n\n    def validate(self, record: EventRecord) -> ValidationResult:\n        start = time.perf_counter()\n        ok = True\n        errors: List[str] = []\n\n        if record.toxicity is not None and record.toxicity > self._max_toxicity:\n            ok = False\n            errors.append(\n                f\"Toxicity {record.toxicity:.2%} exceeds policy limit \"\n                f\"{self._max_toxicity:.2%}\"\n            )\n\n        duration_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000)\n        return ValidationResult(\n            strategy_name=self.name, ok=ok, errors=errors, duration_ms=duration_ms\n        )\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Observer Pattern – side-effect subscribers\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\nclass ValidationEvent(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Observable payload fired after each strategy run.\"\"\"\n\n    record: EventRecord\n    result: ValidationResult\n\n\nclass EventObserver(ABC):\n    \"\"\"Subscriber interface.\"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def notify(self, event: ValidationEvent) -> None: ...\n\n\nclass PrometheusObserver(EventObserver):\n    \"\"\"\n    Publishes validation outcomes to Prometheus Counters/Gauges.\n\n    Metrics exported:\n        • pulse_validation_success_total{strategy=\"…\"}\n        • pulse_validation_failure_total{strategy=\"…\"}\n        • pulse_validation_latency_ms{strategy=\"…\"}\n    \"\"\"\n\n    _success: Counter\n    _failure: Counter\n    _latency: Gauge\n\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        if Counter is None:  # pragma: no cover\n            raise RuntimeError(\n                \"prometheus_client not installed. \"\n                \"Install via `pip install prometheus-client` to enable metrics.\"\n            )\n\n        self._success = Counter(\n            \"pulse_validation_success_total\",\n            \"Successful validation results\",\n            [\"strategy\"],\n        )\n        self._failure = Counter(\n            \"pulse_validation_failure_total\",\n            \"Failed validation results\",\n            [\"strategy\"],\n        )\n        self._latency = Gauge(\n            \"pulse_validation_latency_ms\",\n            \"Validation latency (ms)\",\n            [\"strategy\"],\n        )\n\n    def notify(self, event: ValidationEvent) -> None:\n        strat = event.result.strategy_name\n        if event.result.ok:\n            self._success.labels(strategy=strat).inc()\n        else:\n            self._failure.labels(strategy=strat).inc()\n        self._latency.labels(strategy=strat).set(event.result.duration_ms)\n\n\nclass LoggingObserver(EventObserver):\n    \"\"\"Simple observer that logs failed validations.\"\"\"\n\n    def notify(self, event: ValidationEvent) -> None:\n        if event.result.ok:\n            logger.debug(\n                \"Validated record %s via %s in %d ms\",\n                event.record.event_id,\n                event.result.strategy_name,\n                event.result.duration_ms,\n            )\n            return\n\n        logger.warning(\n            \"Validation failed for %s via %s (%s)\",\n            event.record.event_id,\n            event.result.strategy_name,\n            \"; \".join(event.result.errors),\n        )\n\n\nclass SentryObserver(EventObserver):\n    \"\"\"\n    Ships validation failures to Sentry as breadcrumb + exception.\n\n    The observer is enabled only when sentry_sdk has been initialised.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        if sentry_sdk is None:  # pragma: no cover\n            raise RuntimeError(\n                \"sentry-sdk not installed. Install via `pip install sentry-sdk`.\"\n            )\n\n    def notify(self, event: ValidationEvent) -> None:\n        if event.result.ok:\n            return\n\n        sentry_sdk.add_breadcrumb(\n            category=\"data_validation\",\n            message=f\"Validation errors: {event.result.errors}\",\n            level=\"warning\",\n            data={\"strategy\": event.result.strategy_name},\n        )\n        sentry_sdk.capture_message(\n            f\"[PulseStream] Validation failed for record {event.record.event_id}\",\n            level=\"warning\",\n        )\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# DataQualityMonitor (Observable)                                            #\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\nclass DataQualityMonitor:\n    \"\"\"\n    Drives validation strategy execution and publishes `ValidationEvent`s.\n\n    Typical lifecycle:\n\n        monitor = DataQualityMonitor(\n            strategies=[GreatExpectationsStrategy(), ToxicityBoundsStrategy()],\n            observers=[PrometheusObserver(), LoggingObserver()]\n        )\n        monitor.process(record)  # Returns aggregated success bool\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        strategies: Sequence[BaseValidationStrategy] | None = None,\n        observers: Sequence[EventObserver] | None = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._strategies: Tuple[BaseValidationStrategy, ...] = (\n            tuple(strategies) if strategies else (ToxicityBoundsStrategy(),)\n        )\n        self._observers: Tuple[EventObserver, ...] = (\n            tuple(observers) if observers else (LoggingObserver(),)\n        )\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    def process(self, record: EventRecord) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"\n        Validate a single record using the configured strategies.\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        bool\n            True if the record passes *all* strategies; False otherwise.\n        \"\"\"\n        all_ok = True\n        for strat in self._strategies:\n            result = strat.validate(record)\n            event = ValidationEvent(record=record, result=result)\n\n            # Notify subscribers\n            for observer in self._observers:\n                try:\n                    observer.notify(event)\n                except Exception:  # pragma: no cover – isolating failures\n                    logger.exception(\"Observer %s crashed\", observer.__class__.__name__)\n\n            if not result.ok:\n                all_ok = False\n\n        return all_ok\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Convenience helpers\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\ndef json_stream_reader(\n    source: Iterable[str] | Generator[str, None, None],\n) -> Generator[EventRecord, None, None]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Lazily parse an iterable/stream of JSON lines into validated EventRecord objects.\n\n    Invalid rows are skipped but logged; backpressure is delegated to caller.\n    \"\"\"\n    for raw in source:\n        raw = raw.strip()\n        if not raw:\n            continue\n\n        try:\n            payload = json.loads(raw)\n            yield EventRecord.model_validate(payload)\n        except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValidationError) as exc:\n            logger.error(\"Skipping invalid message: %s – %r\", exc, raw)\n\n\ndef process_event_stream(\n    raw_stream: Iterable[str] | Generator[str, None, None],\n    monitor: Optional[DataQualityMonitor] = None,\n) -> Generator[Tuple[EventRecord, bool], None, None]:\n    \"\"\"\n    High-level generator that (1) parses JSON, (2) validates, (3) yields verdict.\n\n    This function represents the Clean-Architecture *interface adapter*; it is\n    agnostic to message bus / socket / kafka consumer.\n    \"\"\"\n    monitor = monitor or DataQualityMonitor()\n    for record in json_stream_reader(raw_stream):\n        result = monitor.process(record)\n        yield record, result\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Context Manager – benchmark & graceful teardown\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\nclass _StreamTimer:\n    \"\"\"Private context manager used by `__main__` demonstration.\"\"\"\n\n    def __enter__(self):  # noqa: D401\n        self._start = time.perf_counter()\n        return self\n\n    def __exit__(\n        self,\n        exc_type: Optional[Type[BaseException]],\n        exc: Optional[BaseException],\n        tb: Optional[TracebackType],\n    ):\n        took_ms = (time.perf_counter() - self._start) * 1000\n        logger.info(\"Processed stream in %.2f ms\", took_ms)\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Inline usage example                                                       #\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":  # pragma: no cover\n    # Initialize Sentry only when DSN provided\n    if sentry_sdk and (dsn := os.getenv(\"SENTRY_DSN\")):\n        sentry_sdk.init(dsn=dsn, traces_sample_rate=0.05)\n\n    # Synthetic JSONL dataset\n    sample_stream = (\n        json.dumps(  # world!\",\n                \"created_at\": datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n                \"toxicity\": 0.1 * (i % 4),\n            }\n        )\n        for i in range(1, 11)\n    )\n\n    # Instantiate monitor with default strategies + observers\n    dq_monitor = DataQualityMonitor(\n        strategies=[ToxicityBoundsStrategy(max_toxicity=0.3)],\n        observers=[LoggingObserver()],\n    )\n\n    # Run pipeline\n    with _StreamTimer():\n        for rec, verdict in process_event_stream(sample_stream, dq_monitor):\n            logger.info(\n                \"Record %s -> %s\", rec.event_id, \"ACCEPT\" if verdict else \"REJECT\"\n            )\n```","numLines":541,"startLine":1,"totalLines":541}}}

start\n        if _REQUEST_LATENCY:\n            _REQUEST_LATENCY.labels(service, method).observe(duration)\n        if _REQUEST_COUNT:\n            status = \"error\" if exc else \"success\"\n            _REQUEST_COUNT.labels(service, method, status).inc()\n        logger.debug(\n            \"Metrics[%s.%s] duration=%.4fs status=%s\",\n            service,\n            method,\n            duration,\n            \"error\" if exc else \"success\",\n        )\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Miscellaneous helpers\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\ndef chunked(iterable: Iterable[T], size: int) -> Iterator[List[T]]:  # noqa: D401\n    \"\"\"\n    Break *iterable* into fixed-size lists.\n\n    Example\n    -------\n        for page in chunked(users, 100):\n            process(page)\n    \"\"\"\n    if size <= 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"size must be > 0\")\n    it = iter(iterable)\n    while chunk := list(islice(it, size)):\n        yield chunk\n\n\ndef stable_hash(value: Any, *, salt: str = \"\") -> str:  # noqa: D401\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute a stable, short BLAKE2b hash for *value* (used for idempotency keys).\n    \"\"\"\n    h = blake2b(digest_size=16, person=salt.encode(\"utf-8\"))\n    h.update(json.dumps(value, cls=_PulseJSONEncoder, sort_keys=True).encode(\"utf-8\"))\n    return h.hexdigest()\n\n\ndef signal_safe_shutdown(loop: Optional[asyncio.AbstractEventLoop] = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Register SIGINT/SIGTERM handlers so that *asyncio* applications can shut\n    down gracefully.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    async def _shutdown(sig: signal.Signals) -> None:  # noqa: D401\n        logger.info(\"Received signal: %s. Cancelling outstanding tasks...\", sig.name)\n        tasks = [t for t in asyncio.all_tasks(loop) if t is not asyncio.current_task()]\n        [task.cancel() for task in tasks]\n        await asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=True)\n        loop.stop()\n\n    loop = loop or asyncio.get_event_loop()\n    for sig in (signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGINT):\n        loop.add_signal_handler(\n            sig,\n            lambda s=sig: asyncio.create_task(_shutdown(s)),  # noqa: B023\n        )\n\n\ndef setup_sentry(dsn: Optional[str] = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Initialize Sentry (if available) with the current release + python version.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not _HAS_SENTRY:\n        logger.debug(\"Sentry SDK not installed; skipping initialization.\")\n        return\n\n    dsn = dsn or settings.SENTRY_DSN\n    if not dsn:\n        logger.debug(\"No SENTRY_DSN configured; skipping Sentry startup.\")\n        return\n\n    sentry_sdk.init(\n        dsn=dsn,\n        traces_sample_rate=0.05,\n        environment=os.getenv(\"ENVIRONMENT\", \"dev\"),\n        release=f\"PulseStream@{os.getenv('GIT_COMMIT', 'local')}\",\n    )\n    logger.info(\"Sentry initialized.\")\n\n\ndef validate_callable_signature(fn: Callable[..., Any], expected: int | None = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Ensure a callable's signature matches *expected* number of parameters.\n\n    Raises ValueError if the expectation isn't met.  Useful for pluggable\n    strategy validation where duck-typing isn't obvious.\n    \"\"\"\n    params = signature(fn).parameters\n    if expected is not None and len(params) != expected:\n        raise ValueError(\n            f\"Callable {fn.__name__} must accept {expected} parameters \"\n            f\"but has {len(params)} ({list(params)})\"\n        )\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# __all__\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n__all__ = [\n    # config\n    \"settings\",\n    \"Settings\",\n    # json\n    \"dumps\",\n    \"loads\",\n    # retry\n    \"retry\",\n    \"async_retry\",\n    \"ExponentialBackoff\",\n    # metrics\n    \"record_metrics\",\n    # misc\n    \"chunked\",\n    \"stable_hash\",\n    \"signal_safe_shutdown\",\n    \"setup_sentry\",\n    \"validate_callable_signature\",\n]\n```","numLines":538,"startLine":1,"totalLines":538}}}

Module 30 - The Monolithic Bottleneck\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/src/module_30.py\n// Lines: 1-33 (docstring describing tight coupling)\n\n\"\"\"\nPulseStream Nexus – Backfill Orchestrator\n=========================================\nsrc/module_30.py\n\nThis module coordinates historical back-fill jobs that hydrate the data-lake with\nolder social data while guaranteeing data-quality and operational visibility.\n\nKey responsibilities\n--------------------\n1. Strategy Pattern:\n   • Select between multiple execution engines (Spark or Beam).\n\n2. Observer Pattern:\n   • Notify interested observers (e.g. monitoring, alerting) of pipeline events.\n\n3. Validation Layer:\n   • Run Great Expectations suites before persisting data.\n\n4. Monitoring:\n   • Expose Prometheus metrics for job duration, success/failure, and row counts.\n\n5. Scheduling:\n   • Register cron-like schedules through APScheduler (fallback to naïve scheduler\n     when APScheduler is unavailable).\n\"\"\"\n```\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/src/module_30.py\n// Lines: 393-444 (BackfillRunner - the core orchestrator demonstrating tight coupling)\n\nclass BackfillRunner:\n    \"\"\"\n    High-level façade coordinating strategy selection, validation, metrics and\n    observer notifications.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    _ENGINES: Dict[str, BackfillStrategy] = {\n        \"spark\": SparkBackfillStrategy(),\n        \"beam\": BeamBackfillStrategy(),\n    }\n\n    def __init__(self, observer: Optional[PipelineObserver] = None) -> None:\n        self._observer = observer or CompositeObserver(LoggingObserver())\n\n    def run(self, config: BackfillJobConfig) -> int:\n        job_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n        ctx = BackfillContext(job_id=job_id, config=config)\n\n        strategy = self._select_strategy(config.engine)\n\n        # Metrics – status: Pending\n        _METRIC_JOB_STATUS.labels(config.job_name, config.engine).set(0)\n\n        self._notify(\"JOB_STARTED\", ctx)\n        _METRIC_JOB_STATUS.labels(config.job_name, config.engine).set(1)  # Running\n\n        start_time = time.perf_counter()\n        try:\n            processed_rows = strategy.run(ctx)  # INGESTION + TRANSFORM\n            ctx.rows_processed = processed_rows\n            _run_expectation_suite(ctx)  # VALIDATION\n\n            # Metrics\n            duration = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n            _METRIC_JOB_DURATION.labels(config.job_name, config.engine).observe(duration)\n            _METRIC_JOB_ROWS.labels(config.job_name, config.engine).inc(processed_rows)\n            _METRIC_JOB_STATUS.labels(config.job_name, config.engine).set(2)  # Success\n\n            self._notify(\"JOB_SUCCEEDED\", ctx, payload={\"rows\": processed_rows})\n            return processed_rows\n```\n\n### 2. BackfillJobConfig - Tightly Coupled Configuration\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/src/module_30.py\n// Lines: 126-141\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass BackfillJobConfig:\n    \"\"\"\n    Immutable configuration required to run a back-fill job.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    job_name: str\n    source_uri: str  # e.g., \"s3://pulstream/stage/twitter/2023/*/*.json\"\n    target_table: str\n    engine: str  # e.g. 'spark', 'beam'\n    schedule_cron: Optional[str] = None  # e.g. '0 2 * * *'\n    expectation_suite_name: Optional[str] = None\n    extra_options: MappingProxyType[str, Any] = field(\n        default_factory=lambda: MappingProxyType({})\n    )\n```\n\n### 3. Validation Logic in Module 17 (Reusable)\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/src/module_17.py\n// Lines: 103-179\n\nclass DataValidator:\n    \"\"\"\n    Lightweight validator that falls back to a manual schema when Great\n    Expectations is unavailable.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    DEFAULT_SCHEMA: Dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"id\": str,\n        \"source\": str,\n        \"timestamp\": (int, float),\n        \"payload\": dict,\n    }\n\n    def __init__(self, schema: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> None:\n        self._schema = schema or self.DEFAULT_SCHEMA\n\n    def _validate_static_schema(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\n        for field, expected in self._schema.items():\n            if field not in data:\n                return False\n            if not isinstance(data[field], expected):\n                return False\n        return True\n\n    def validate(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\n        if ge is not None and self._ge_context is not None:\n            return self._validate_with_ge(data)\n        return self._validate_static_schema(data)\n```\n\n### 4. Transformation Strategies in Module 8 (Reusable)\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/src/module_8.py\n// Lines: 156-223\n\nclass TransformStrategy(ABC):\n    \"\"\"\n    Strategy interface for mutating an Event in place or returning an\n    enriched copy.  Implementation must not perform blocking I/O.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    name: str\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def transform(self, event: Event) -> Event:\n        \"\"\"Return a new, transformed Event instance.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass SentimentTransform(TransformStrategy):\n    name = \"sentiment\"\n    # ... implementation\n\nclass ToxicityTransform(TransformStrategy):\n    name = \"toxicity\"\n    # ... implementation\n\nclass ViralityTransform(TransformStrategy):\n    name = \"virality\"\n    # ... implementation\n```\n\n### 5. Current Config Structure (Linear Pipeline Focus)\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/src/config.py\n// Lines: 73-118\n\nclass KafkaSettings(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Kafka-specific configuration.\"\"\"\n\n    bootstrap_servers: str = Field(..., env=\"KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS\")\n    consumer_group: str = Field(\"pulse_stream_consumer\", env=\"KAFKA_CONSUMER_GROUP\")\n    input_topics: List[str] = Field(\n        default_factory=lambda: [\n            \"twitter_raw\",\n            \"reddit_raw\",\n            \"mastodon_raw\",\n            \"discord_raw\",\n        ],\n        env=\"KAFKA_INPUT_TOPICS\",\n    )\n    output_topic_enriched: str = Field(\"social_events_enriched\", env=\"KAFKA_OUT_ENRICHED\")\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### 1. Component Identification: The Monolithic Bottleneck\n\n**`module_30.py` (BackfillRunner)** is the primary bottleneck for real-time integration. This module is a **god module** that handles:\n\n- **Data Ingestion**: Fetches data from S3 via Spark/Beam strategies\n- **Validation**: Runs Great Expectations suites inline during job execution\n- **Transformation**: Applies transformations through the strategy's `run()` method\n- **Scheduling**: Manages cron-like scheduling via APScheduler\n- **Metrics Collection**: Directly emits Prometheus metrics\n- **Observer Notifications**: Notifies observers of pipeline events\n\n**Why This Is a Bottleneck for Real-Time Integration:**\n\n1. **Synchronous, Batch-Oriented Design**: The `BackfillRunner.run()` method is designed for batch jobs that complete in a single execution cycle. It cannot handle continuous, unbounded streams of data.\n\n2. **Tight Coupling of Ingestion and Validation**: The validation (`_run_expectation_suite`) is called immediately after ingestion within the same method, making it impossible to decouple these concerns for different latency requirements.\n\n3. **Source-Specific Strategy Selection**: The `BackfillStrategy` interface assumes batch sources (S3 file globs). Real-time sources (Kafka streams) have fundamentally different consumption patterns (continuous polling vs. finite file listing).\n\n4. **Blocking Execution Model**: The `BackfillRunner` blocks until the entire batch is processed. Real-time streams require non-blocking, event-driven processing.\n\n5. **Configuration Lock-in**: `BackfillJobConfig` is designed for batch jobs with `source_uri` pointing to S3 paths, not Kafka topic subscriptions.\n\n### 2. Core Architectural Flaw\n\nThe core architectural flaw is the **tight coupling between ingestion, validation, and transformation** within a single execution context. In `module_30.py`:\n\n```\n[S3 Source] → [Spark/Beam Strategy.run()] → [GE Validation] → [Metrics/Notify]\n              ↑___________________________________↑\n              All happens in one synchronous call\n```\n\nThis makes it impossible to:\n- Process data from different source types (batch vs. real-time) through a unified pipeline\n- Scale validation independently of ingestion\n- Apply different SLAs to batch vs. real-time processing\n- Handle backpressure from real-time sources\n\n### 3. Target Architecture Proposal: Event-Driven Architecture with Message Queue\n\nI propose refactoring to an **Event-Driven Architecture (EDA)** using a message queue (Kafka) as the central nervous system. This pattern decouples services through asynchronous message passing, enabling both batch and real-time sources to coexist.\n\n#### New Architecture Components:\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                        EVENT-DRIVEN ARCHITECTURE                             │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                                             │\n│  ┌─────────────┐    ┌─────────────┐                                        │\n│  │ S3 Batch    │    │ Kafka       │                                        │\n│  │ Source      │    │ Real-Time   │                                        │\n│  └──────┬──────┘    └──────┬──────┘                                        │\n│         │                  │                                                │\n│         ▼                  ▼                                                │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐                                   │\n│  │      INGESTION SERVICE              │  (Single Responsibility:          │\n│  │  • BatchIngestionAdapter            │   Source Connection Only)         │\n│  │  • StreamIngestionAdapter           │                                   │\n│  └──────────────────┬──────────────────┘                                   │\n│                     │                                                       │\n│                     ▼                                                       │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐                                   │\n│  │     Kafka Topic: raw_events         │  (Canonical Event Format)         │\n│  └──────────────────┬──────────────────┘                                   │\n│                     │                                                       │\n│                     ▼ │  • DataValidator (module_17)        │   Schema & GE Validation)         │\n│  │  • DataQualityMonitor (module_37)   │                                   │\n│  └──────────────────┬──────────────────┘                                   │\n│                     │                                                       │\n│          ┌─────────┴─────────┐                                             │\n│          ▼                   ▼                                             │\n│  ┌───────────────┐   ┌───────────────┐                                     │\n│  │ valid_events  │   │ invalid_events│  (Dead Letter Queue)                │\n│  │ (Kafka Topic) │   │ (Kafka Topic) │                                     │\n│  └───────┬───────┘   └───────────────┘                                     │\n│          │                                                                  │\n│          ▼                                                                  │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐                                   │\n│  │      PROCESSING SERVICE             │  (Single Responsibility:          │\n│  │  • ProcessingPipeline (module_8)    │   Transformation Only)            │\n│  │  • SentimentTransform               │                                   │\n│  │  • ToxicityTransform                │                                   │\n│  │  • ViralityTransform                │                                   │\n│  └──────────────────┬──────────────────┘                                   │\n│                     │                                                       │\n│                     ▼                                                       │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐                                   │\n│  │  Kafka Topic: enriched_events       │                                   │\n│  └──────────────────┬──────────────────┘                                   │\n│                     │                                                       │\n│                     ▼                                                       │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐                                   │\n│  │      SINK SERVICE                   │  (Single Responsibility:          │\n│  │  • Data Lake Writer (S3/Delta)      │   Persistence Only)               │\n│  │  • Warehouse Writer (Snowflake)     │                                   │\n│  └─────────────────────────────────────┘                                   │\n│                                                                             │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 4. Logic Mapping: Existing Code to New Services\n\n| New Service | Source Module | Classes/Functions to Migrate |\n|-------------|---------------|------------------------------|\n| **Ingestion Service** | `module_30.py` | `SparkBackfillStrategy.run()` (batch), `BackfillJobConfig` (adapted) |\n| **Ingestion Service** | `module_17.py` | `StreamProcessor._setup()`, `StreamProcessor._consume()` (real-time) |\n| **Validation Service** | `module_17.py` | `DataValidator`, `DataValidator.validate()`, `DataValidator._validate_static_schema()` |\n| **Validation Service** | `module_37.py` | `DataQualityMonitor`, `BaseValidationStrategy`, `ToxicityBoundsStrategy`, `GreatExpectationsStrategy` |\n| **Processing Service** | `module_8.py` | `ProcessingPipeline`, `TransformStrategy`, `SentimentTransform`, `ToxicityTransform`, `ViralityTransform` |\n| **Processing Service** | `module_17.py` | `BaseTransformer`, `SentimentTransformer`, `ToxicityTransformer`, `ViralityTransformer` |\n| **Cross-Cutting** | `utils.py` | `ExponentialBackoff`, `retry`, `async_retry`, `record_metrics`, `chunked` |\n| **Observability** | `module_8.py` | `EventObserver`, `LoggingObserver`, `MetricsObserver` |\n| **Observability** | `module_37.py` | `PrometheusObserver`, `LoggingObserver`, `SentryObserver` |\n\n### 5. Data Flow Explanation\n\n#### Batch Data Flow (S3 Source)\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant S3 as S3 Bucket\n    participant BI as Batch Ingestion Adapter\n    participant KR as Kafka: raw_events\n    participant VS as Validation Service\n    participant KV as Kafka: valid_events\n    participant PS as Processing Service\n    participant KE as Kafka: enriched_events\n    participant DL as Data Lake (S3/Delta)\n\n    Note over S3,DL: Batch Flow (Scheduled, e.g., daily at 02:00 UTC)\n\n    BI->>S3: List files matching s3://bucket/2023/*/*.json\n    S3-->>BI: File manifest\n    loop For each file chunk\n        BI->>S3: Read JSON records\n        S3-->>BI: Records\n        BI->>KR: Produce raw events (with source_type=batch)\n    end\n\n    VS->>KR: Consume raw events\n    KR-->>VS: Event batch\n    VS->>VS: Validate schema + GE expectations\n    alt Valid\n        VS->>KV: Produce to valid_events\n    else Invalid\n        VS->>KV: Produce to invalid_events (DLQ)\n    end\n\n    PS->>KV: Consume valid events\n    KV-->>PS: Valid events\n    PS->>PS: Apply transformations (sentiment, toxicity, virality)\n    PS->>KE: Produce enriched events\n\n    DL->>KE: Consume enriched events\n    DL->>DL: Write to Delta Lake / S3 Parquet\n```\n\n#### Real-Time Data Flow (Kafka Stream Source)\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant EX as External Kafka Stream\n    participant SI as Stream Ingestion Adapter\n    participant KR as Kafka: raw_events\n    participant VS as Validation Service\n    participant KV as Kafka: valid_events\n    participant PS as Processing Service\n    participant KE as Kafka: enriched_events\n    participant RT as Real-Time Consumers\n\n    Note over EX,RT: Real-Time Flow (Continuous)\n\n    loop Continuous polling\n        SI->>EX: Subscribe to twitter_raw, reddit_raw, etc.\n        EX-->>SI: Stream record\n        SI->>SI: Normalize to canonical format\n        SI->>KR: Produce raw event (with source_type=stream)\n    end\n\n    loop Continuous processing\n        VS->>KR: Consume raw events (microsecond latency)\n        KR-->>VS: Event\n        VS->>VS: Fast validation (static schema)\n        alt Valid\n            VS->>KV: Produce to valid_events\n        else Invalid\n            VS->>KV: Produce to invalid_events (DLQ)\n        end\n    end\n\n    loop Continuous enrichment\n        PS->>KV: Consume valid events\n        KV-->>PS: Event\n        PS->>PS: Apply real-time transformations\n        PS->>KE: Produce enriched event\n    end\n\n    RT->>KE: Subscribe to enriched_events\n    KE-->>RT: Enriched event for dashboards, alerts, etc.\n```\n\n#### Unified Data Flow (Text Description)\n\n1. **Ingestion Layer**:\n   - **Batch Path**: Scheduled job reads from S3, normalizes records to canonical format, produces to `raw_events` topic\n   - **Real-Time Path**: Kafka consumer subscribes to external topics (twitter_raw, etc.), normalizes to canonical format, produces to `raw_events` topic\n   - Both paths converge at the same `raw_events` topic\n\n2. **Validation Layer**:\n   - Consumes from `raw_events` topic\n   - Applies validation strategies (schema validation, Great Expectations, domain rules)\n   - Routes valid events to `valid_events` topic\n   - Routes invalid events to `invalid_events` dead-letter queue\n\n3. **Processing Layer**:\n   - Consumes from `valid_events` topic\n   - Applies transformation pipeline (sentiment, toxicity, virality enrichment)\n   - Produces enriched events to `enriched_events` topic\n\n4. **Sink Layer**:\n   - Batch consumers write to Data Lake (Delta Lake, Parquet)\n   - Real-time consumers feed dashboards, alerts, and analytics\n\n### 6. Configuration Impact Analysis\n\nThe current `src/config.py` defines configuration for a **monolithic, linear pipeline**. It must shift to configure **independent services and their communication channels**.\n\n#### Current Configuration Structure (Linear Pipeline)\n\n```python\nclass AppSettings(BaseSettings):\n    kafka: KafkaSettings      # Single Kafka config for entire app\n    db: DatabaseSettings      # Single DB config\n    spark: SparkSettings      # Batch job config\n```\n\n#### Required Configuration Changes\n\n```python\n# Conceptual new configuration structure\n\nclass ServiceDiscoverySettings(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Service mesh / discovery configuration\"\"\"\n    consul_host: str\n    consul_port: int\n\nclass MessageBrokerSettings(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Central message broker (Kafka) configuration\"\"\"\n    bootstrap_servers: str\n    schema_registry_url: str\n    security_protocol: str\n\n    # Topic definitions - the contract between services\n    raw_events_topic: str = \"raw_events\"\n    valid_events_topic: str = \"valid_events\"\n    invalid_events_topic: str = \"invalid_events\"  # DLQ\n    enriched_events_topic: str = \"enriched_events\"\n\nclass IngestionServiceSettings(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Configuration specific to Ingestion Service\"\"\"\n    service_name: str = \"ingestion-service\"\n\n    # Batch source configuration\n    batch_sources: List[BatchSourceConfig]  # S3 URIs, schedules\n\n    # Stream source configuration\n    stream_sources: List[StreamSourceConfig]  # External Kafka topics\n\n    # Output topic (where to publish raw events)\n    output_topic: str = \"raw_events\"\n\n    consumer_group: str = \"ingestion-service-cg\"\n\nclass ValidationServiceSettings(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Configuration specific to Validation Service\"\"\"\n    service_name: str = \"validation-service\"\n\n    # Input/output topics\n    input_topic: str = \"raw_events\"\n    valid_output_topic: str = \"valid_events\"\n    invalid_output_topic: str = \"invalid_events\"\n\n    # Validation strategies to apply\n    strategies: List[str] = [\"schema\", \"great_expectations\", \"toxicity_bounds\"]\n\n    # GE configuration\n    expectation_suite_name: str = \"pulstream_core_stream_events\"\n\n    consumer_group: str = \"validation-service-cg\"\n\nclass ProcessingServiceSettings(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Configuration specific to Processing Service\"\"\"\n    service_name: str = \"processing-service\"\n\n    # Input/output topics\n    input_topic: str = \"valid_events\"\n    output_topic: str = \"enriched_events\"\n\n    # Transformation strategies to apply\n    transformers: List[str] = [\"sentiment\", \"toxicity\", \"virality\"]\n\n    # Concurrency settings\n    max_concurrency: int = 64\n\n    consumer_group: str = \"processing-service-cg\"\n\nclass SinkServiceSettings(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Configuration specific to Sink/Persistence Service\"\"\"\n    service_name: str = \"sink-service\"\n\n    input_topic: str = \"enriched_events\"\n\n    # Data Lake configuration\n    data_lake_path: str = \"s3://pulstream/lake/\"\n    data_lake_format: str = \"delta\"\n\n    consumer_group: str = \"sink-service-cg\"\n\n# Root configuration now composes service configs\nclass PulseStreamNexusSettings(BaseSettings):\n    \"\"\"Root configuration for the entire platform\"\"\"\n    env: str = \"development\"\n\n    # Shared infrastructure\n    message_broker: MessageBrokerSettings\n    service_discovery: ServiceDiscoverySettings\n    monitoring: MonitoringSettings\n\n    # Per-service configurations (deployed independently)\n    ingestion: IngestionServiceSettings\n    validation: ValidationServiceSettings\n    processing: ProcessingServiceSettings\n    sink: SinkServiceSettings\n```\n\n#### Key Configuration Shifts:\n\n1. **From Single Pipeline to Service Mesh**: Configuration moves from defining a single linear pipeline to defining independent services with their own lifecycles.\n\n2. **Topic-Based Contracts**: Services communicate through well-defined Kafka topics. The topic names become the API contract between services.\n\n3. **Independent Consumer Groups**: Each service has its own consumer group, allowing independent scaling and replay.\n\n4. **Strategy Configuration Per Service**: Each service declares which strategies/transformers it will apply, loaded from configuration rather than hard-coded.\n\n5. **Source-Agnostic Processing**: The validation and processing services don't know whether data came from batch (S3) or real-time (Kafka) sources - they just consume from the `raw_events` topic.\n\n## Summary\n\n- **Primary finding**: `module_30.py` (BackfillRunner) is the monolithic bottleneck component that tightly couples ingestion, validation, and transformation, making it unsuitable for real-time integration.\n\n- **Key components**:\n  - `/app/project/src/module_30.py` - Monolithic batch orchestrator (bottleneck)\n  - `/app/project/src/module_17.py` - Streaming processor with reusable validation/transformation logic\n  - `/app/project/src/module_8.py` - Processing pipeline with Strategy and Observer patterns\n  - `/app/project/src/module_37.py` - Data quality validation strategies\n  - `/app/project/src/config.py` - Configuration that needs restructuring for microservices\n\n- **Architectural pattern**: **Event-Driven Architecture (EDA)** with Kafka as the message bus, decomposing the monolith into:\n  1. **Ingestion Service** - Handles both batch (S3) and real-time (Kafka) sources\n  2. **Validation Service** - Applies schema and domain validation\n  3. **Processing Service** - Applies enrichment transformations\n  4. **Sink Service** - Persists to data lake/warehouse\n\n- **Recommendations**:\n  1. Refactor `module_30.py` to extract ingestion logic into a dedicated Ingestion Service\n  2. Create a Validation Service reusing `DataValidator` from `module_17.py` and `DataQualityMonitor` from `module_37.py`\n  3. Create a Processing Service reusing `ProcessingPipeline` and `TransformStrategy` implementations from `module_8.py`\n  4. Restructure `config.py` to define service-specific configurations and topic-based contracts\n  5. Introduce a canonical event format that both batch and real-time sources normalize to\n  6. Implement dead-letter queues for failed validation/processing for observability\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":12876,"cache_read_input_tokens":72918,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":12876,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c70c85d0-b2a3-40dc-83cd-e779c2d959af","uuid":"509ca311-b6bd-4ae1-96df-800a37898e4a"}

Module 30 - The Monolithic Bottleneck\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/src/module_30.py\n// Lines: 1-33 (docstring describing tight coupling)\n\n\"\"\"\nPulseStream Nexus – Backfill Orchestrator\n=========================================\nsrc/module_30.py\n\nThis module coordinates historical back-fill jobs that hydrate the data-lake with\nolder social data while guaranteeing data-quality and operational visibility.\n\nKey responsibilities\n--------------------\n1. Strategy Pattern:\n   • Select between multiple execution engines (Spark or Beam).\n\n2. Observer Pattern:\n   • Notify interested observers (e.g. monitoring, alerting) of pipeline events.\n\n3. Validation Layer:\n   • Run Great Expectations suites before persisting data.\n\n4. Monitoring:\n   • Expose Prometheus metrics for job duration, success/failure, and row counts.\n\n5. Scheduling:\n   • Register cron-like schedules through APScheduler (fallback to naïve scheduler\n     when APScheduler is unavailable).\n\"\"\"\n```\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/src/module_30.py\n// Lines: 393-444 (BackfillRunner - the core orchestrator demonstrating tight coupling)\n\nclass BackfillRunner:\n    \"\"\"\n    High-level façade coordinating strategy selection, validation, metrics and\n    observer notifications.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    _ENGINES: Dict[str, BackfillStrategy] = {\n        \"spark\": SparkBackfillStrategy(),\n        \"beam\": BeamBackfillStrategy(),\n    }\n\n    def __init__(self, observer: Optional[PipelineObserver] = None) -> None:\n        self._observer = observer or CompositeObserver(LoggingObserver())\n\n    def run(self, config: BackfillJobConfig) -> int:\n        job_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n        ctx = BackfillContext(job_id=job_id, config=config)\n\n        strategy = self._select_strategy(config.engine)\n\n        # Metrics – status: Pending\n        _METRIC_JOB_STATUS.labels(config.job_name, config.engine).set(0)\n\n        self._notify(\"JOB_STARTED\", ctx)\n        _METRIC_JOB_STATUS.labels(config.job_name, config.engine).set(1)  # Running\n\n        start_time = time.perf_counter()\n        try:\n            processed_rows = strategy.run(ctx)  # INGESTION + TRANSFORM\n            ctx.rows_processed = processed_rows\n            _run_expectation_suite(ctx)  # VALIDATION\n\n            # Metrics\n            duration = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n            _METRIC_JOB_DURATION.labels(config.job_name, config.engine).observe(duration)\n            _METRIC_JOB_ROWS.labels(config.job_name, config.engine).inc(processed_rows)\n            _METRIC_JOB_STATUS.labels(config.job_name, config.engine).set(2)  # Success\n\n            self._notify(\"JOB_SUCCEEDED\", ctx, payload={\"rows\": processed_rows})\n            return processed_rows\n```\n\n### 2. BackfillJobConfig - Tightly Coupled Configuration\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/src/module_30.py\n// Lines: 126-141\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass BackfillJobConfig:\n    \"\"\"\n    Immutable configuration required to run a back-fill job.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    job_name: str\n    source_uri: str  # e.g., \"s3://pulstream/stage/twitter/2023/*/*.json\"\n    target_table: str\n    engine: str  # e.g. 'spark', 'beam'\n    schedule_cron: Optional[str] = None  # e.g. '0 2 * * *'\n    expectation_suite_name: Optional[str] = None\n    extra_options: MappingProxyType[str, Any] = field(\n        default_factory=lambda: MappingProxyType({})\n    )\n```\n\n### 3. Validation Logic in Module 17 (Reusable)\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/src/module_17.py\n// Lines: 103-179\n\nclass DataValidator:\n    \"\"\"\n    Lightweight validator that falls back to a manual schema when Great\n    Expectations is unavailable.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    DEFAULT_SCHEMA: Dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"id\": str,\n        \"source\": str,\n        \"timestamp\": (int, float),\n        \"payload\": dict,\n    }\n\n    def __init__(self, schema: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> None:\n        self._schema = schema or self.DEFAULT_SCHEMA\n\n    def _validate_static_schema(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\n        for field, expected in self._schema.items():\n            if field not in data:\n                return False\n            if not isinstance(data[field], expected):\n                return False\n        return True\n\n    def validate(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\n        if ge is not None and self._ge_context is not None:\n            return self._validate_with_ge(data)\n        return self._validate_static_schema(data)\n```\n\n### 4. Transformation Strategies in Module 8 (Reusable)\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/src/module_8.py\n// Lines: 156-223\n\nclass TransformStrategy(ABC):\n    \"\"\"\n    Strategy interface for mutating an Event in place or returning an\n    enriched copy.  Implementation must not perform blocking I/O.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    name: str\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def transform(self, event: Event) -> Event:\n        \"\"\"Return a new, transformed Event instance.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass SentimentTransform(TransformStrategy):\n    name = \"sentiment\"\n    # ... implementation\n\nclass ToxicityTransform(TransformStrategy):\n    name = \"toxicity\"\n    # ... implementation\n\nclass ViralityTransform(TransformStrategy):\n    name = \"virality\"\n    # ... implementation\n```\n\n### 5. Current Config Structure (Linear Pipeline Focus)\n\n```python\n// File: /app/project/src/config.py\n// Lines: 73-118\n\nclass KafkaSettings(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Kafka-specific configuration.\"\"\"\n\n    bootstrap_servers: str = Field(..., env=\"KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS\")\n    consumer_group: str = Field(\"pulse_stream_consumer\", env=\"KAFKA_CONSUMER_GROUP\")\n    input_topics: List[str] = Field(\n        default_factory=lambda: [\n            \"twitter_raw\",\n            \"reddit_raw\",\n            \"mastodon_raw\",\n            \"discord_raw\",\n        ],\n        env=\"KAFKA_INPUT_TOPICS\",\n    )\n    output_topic_enriched: str = Field(\"social_events_enriched\", env=\"KAFKA_OUT_ENRICHED\")\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### 1. Component Identification: The Monolithic Bottleneck\n\n**`module_30.py` (BackfillRunner)** is the primary bottleneck for real-time integration. This module is a **god module** that handles:\n\n- **Data Ingestion**: Fetches data from S3 via Spark/Beam strategies\n- **Validation**: Runs Great Expectations suites inline during job execution\n- **Transformation**: Applies transformations through the strategy's `run()` method\n- **Scheduling**: Manages cron-like scheduling via APScheduler\n- **Metrics Collection**: Directly emits Prometheus metrics\n- **Observer Notifications**: Notifies observers of pipeline events\n\n**Why This Is a Bottleneck for Real-Time Integration:**\n\n1. **Synchronous, Batch-Oriented Design**: The `BackfillRunner.run()` method is designed for batch jobs that complete in a single execution cycle. It cannot handle continuous, unbounded streams of data.\n\n2. **Tight Coupling of Ingestion and Validation**: The validation (`_run_expectation_suite`) is called immediately after ingestion within the same method, making it impossible to decouple these concerns for different latency requirements.\n\n3. **Source-Specific Strategy Selection**: The `BackfillStrategy` interface assumes batch sources (S3 file globs). Real-time sources (Kafka streams) have fundamentally different consumption patterns (continuous polling vs. finite file listing).\n\n4. **Blocking Execution Model**: The `BackfillRunner` blocks until the entire batch is processed. Real-time streams require non-blocking, event-driven processing.\n\n5. **Configuration Lock-in**: `BackfillJobConfig` is designed for batch jobs with `source_uri` pointing to S3 paths, not Kafka topic subscriptions.\n\n### 2. Core Architectural Flaw\n\nThe core architectural flaw is the **tight coupling between ingestion, validation, and transformation** within a single execution context. In `module_30.py`:\n\n```\n[S3 Source] → [Spark/Beam Strategy.run()] → [GE Validation] → [Metrics/Notify]\n              ↑___________________________________↑\n              All happens in one synchronous call\n```\n\nThis makes it impossible to:\n- Process data from different source types (batch vs. real-time) through a unified pipeline\n- Scale validation independently of ingestion\n- Apply different SLAs to batch vs. real-time processing\n- Handle backpressure from real-time sources\n\n### 3. Target Architecture Proposal: Event-Driven Architecture with Message Queue\n\nI propose refactoring to an **Event-Driven Architecture (EDA)** using a message queue (Kafka) as the central nervous system. This pattern decouples services through asynchronous message passing, enabling both batch and real-time sources to coexist.\n\n#### New Architecture Components:\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                        EVENT-DRIVEN ARCHITECTURE                             │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                                             │\n│  ┌─────────────┐    ┌─────────────┐                                        │\n│  │ S3 Batch    │    │ Kafka       │                                        │\n│  │ Source      │    │ Real-Time   │                                        │\n│  └──────┬──────┘    └──────┬──────┘                                        │\n│         │                  │                                                │\n│         ▼                  ▼                                                │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐                                   │\n│  │      INGESTION SERVICE              │  (Single Responsibility:          │\n│  │  • BatchIngestionAdapter            │   Source Connection Only)         │\n│  │  • StreamIngestionAdapter           │                                   │\n│  └──────────────────┬──────────────────┘                                   │\n│                     │                                                       │\n│                     ▼                                                       │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐                                   │\n│  │     Kafka Topic: raw_events         │  (Canonical Event Format)         │\n│  └──────────────────┬──────────────────┘                                   │\n│                     │                                                       │\n│                     ▼                                                       │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐                                   │\n│  │      VALIDATION SERVICE             │  (Single Responsibility:          │\n│  │  • DataValidator (module_17)        │   Schema & GE Validation)         │\n│  │  • DataQualityMonitor (module_37)   │                                   │\n│  └──────────────────┬──────────────────┘                                   │\n│                     │                                                       │\n│          ┌─────────┴─────────┐                                             │\n│          ▼                   ▼                                             │\n│  ┌───────────────┐   ┌───────────────┐                                     │\n│  │ valid_events  │   │ invalid_events│  (Dead Letter Queue)                │\n│  │ (Kafka Topic) │   │ (Kafka Topic) │                                     │\n│  └───────┬───────┘   └───────────────┘                                     │\n│          │                                                                  │\n│          ▼                                                                  │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐                                   │\n│  │      PROCESSING SERVICE             │  (Single Responsibility:          │\n│  │  • ProcessingPipeline (module_8)    │   Transformation Only)            │\n│  │  • SentimentTransform               │                                   │\n│  │  • ToxicityTransform                │                                   │\n│  │  • ViralityTransform                │                                   │\n│  └──────────────────┬──────────────────┘                                   │\n│                     │                                                       │\n│                     ▼                                                       │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐                                   │\n│  │  Kafka Topic: enriched_events       │                                   │\n│  └──────────────────┬──────────────────┘                                   │\n│                     │                                                       │\n│                     ▼                                                       │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐                                   │\n│  │      SINK SERVICE                   │  (Single Responsibility:          │\n│  │  • Data Lake Writer (S3/Delta)      │   Persistence Only)               │\n│  │  • Warehouse Writer (Snowflake)     │                                   │\n│  └─────────────────────────────────────┘                                   │\n│                                                                             │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 4. Logic Mapping: Existing Code to New Services\n\n| New Service | Source Module | Classes/Functions to Migrate |\n|-------------|---------------|------------------------------|\n| **Ingestion Service** | `module_30.py` | `SparkBackfillStrategy.run()` (batch), `BackfillJobConfig` (adapted) |\n| **Ingestion Service** | `module_17.py` | `StreamProcessor._setup()`, `StreamProcessor._consume()` (real-time) |\n| **Validation Service** | `module_17.py` | `DataValidator`, `DataValidator.validate()`, `DataValidator._validate_static_schema()` |\n| **Validation Service** | `module_37.py` | `DataQualityMonitor`, `BaseValidationStrategy`, `ToxicityBoundsStrategy`, `GreatExpectationsStrategy` |\n| **Processing Service** | `module_8.py` | `ProcessingPipeline`, `TransformStrategy`, `SentimentTransform`, `ToxicityTransform`, `ViralityTransform` |\n| **Processing Service** | `module_17.py` | `BaseTransformer`, `SentimentTransformer`, `ToxicityTransformer`, `ViralityTransformer` |\n| **Cross-Cutting** | `utils.py` | `ExponentialBackoff`, `retry`, `async_retry`, `record_metrics`, `chunked` |\n| **Observability** | `module_8.py` | `EventObserver`, `LoggingObserver`, `MetricsObserver` |\n| **Observability** | `module_37.py` | `PrometheusObserver`, `LoggingObserver`, `SentryObserver` |\n\n### 5. Data Flow Explanation\n\n#### Batch Data Flow (S3 Source)\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant S3 as S3 Bucket\n    participant BI as Batch Ingestion Adapter\n    participant KR as Kafka: raw_events\n    participant VS as Validation Service\n    participant KV as Kafka: valid_events\n    participant PS as Processing Service\n    participant KE as Kafka: enriched_events\n    participant DL as Data Lake (S3/Delta)\n\n    Note over S3,DL: Batch Flow (Scheduled, e.g., daily at 02:00 UTC)\n\n    BI->>S3: List files matching s3://bucket/2023/*/*.json\n    S3-->>BI: File manifest\n    loop For each file chunk\n        BI->>S3: Read JSON records\n        S3-->>BI: Records\n        BI->>KR: Produce raw events (with source_type=batch)\n    end\n\n    VS->>KR: Consume raw events\n    KR-->>VS: Event batch\n    VS->>VS: Validate schema + GE expectations\n    alt Valid\n        VS->>KV: Produce to valid_events\n    else Invalid\n        VS->>KV: Produce to invalid_events (DLQ)\n    end\n\n    PS->>KV: Consume valid events\n    KV-->>PS: Valid events\n    PS->>PS: Apply transformations (sentiment, toxicity, virality)\n    PS->>KE: Produce enriched events\n\n    DL->>KE: Consume enriched events\n    DL->>DL: Write to Delta Lake / S3 Parquet\n```\n\n#### Real-Time Data Flow (Kafka Stream Source)\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant EX as External Kafka Stream\n    participant SI as Stream Ingestion Adapter\n    participant KR as Kafka: raw_events\n    participant VS as Validation Service\n    participant KV as Kafka: valid_events\n    participant PS as Processing Service\n    participant KE as Kafka: enriched_events\n    participant RT as Real-Time Consumers\n\n    Note over EX,RT: Real-Time Flow (Continuous)\n\n    loop Continuous polling\n        SI->>EX: Subscribe to twitter_raw, reddit_raw, etc.\n        EX-->>SI: Stream record\n        SI->>SI: Normalize to canonical format\n        SI->>KR: Produce raw event (with source_type=stream)\n    end\n\n    loop Continuous processing\n        VS->>KR: Consume raw events (microsecond latency)\n        KR-->>VS: Event\n        VS->>VS: Fast validation (static schema)\n        alt Valid\n            VS->>KV: Produce to valid_events\n        else Invalid\n            VS->>KV: Produce to invalid_events (DLQ)\n        end\n    end\n\n    loop Continuous enrichment\n        PS->>KV: Consume valid events\n        KV-->>PS: Event\n        PS->>PS: Apply real-time transformations\n        PS->>KE: Produce enriched event\n    end\n\n    RT->>KE: Subscribe to enriched_events\n    KE-->>RT: Enriched event for dashboards, alerts, etc.\n```\n\n#### Unified Data Flow (Text Description)\n\n1. **Ingestion Layer**:\n   - **Batch Path**: Scheduled job reads from S3, normalizes records to canonical format, produces to `raw_events` topic\n   - **Real-Time Path**: Kafka consumer subscribes to external topics (twitter_raw, etc.), normalizes to canonical format, produces to `raw_events` topic\n   - Both paths converge at the same `raw_events` topic\n\n2. **Validation Layer**:\n   - Consumes from `raw_events` topic\n   - Applies validation strategies (schema validation, Great Expectations, domain rules)\n   - Routes valid events to `valid_events` topic\n   - Routes invalid events to `invalid_events` dead-letter queue\n\n3. **Processing Layer**:\n   - Consumes from `valid_events` topic\n   - Applies transformation pipeline (sentiment, toxicity, virality enrichment)\n   - Produces enriched events to `enriched_events` topic\n\n4. **Sink Layer**:\n   - Batch consumers write to Data Lake (Delta Lake, Parquet)\n   - Real-time consumers feed dashboards, alerts, and analytics\n\n### 6. Configuration Impact Analysis\n\nThe current `src/config.py` defines configuration for a **monolithic, linear pipeline**. It must shift to configure **independent services and their communication channels**.\n\n#### Current Configuration Structure (Linear Pipeline)\n\n```python\nclass AppSettings(BaseSettings):\n    kafka: KafkaSettings      # Single Kafka config for entire app\n    db: DatabaseSettings      # Single DB config\n    spark: SparkSettings      # Batch job config\n```\n\n#### Required Configuration Changes\n\n```python\n# Conceptual new configuration structure\n\nclass ServiceDiscoverySettings(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Service mesh / discovery configuration\"\"\"\n    consul_host: str\n    consul_port: int\n\nclass MessageBrokerSettings(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Central message broker (Kafka) configuration\"\"\"\n    bootstrap_servers: str\n    schema_registry_url: str\n    security_protocol: str\n\n    # Topic definitions - the contract between services\n    raw_events_topic: str = \"raw_events\"\n    valid_events_topic: str = \"valid_events\"\n    invalid_events_topic: str = \"invalid_events\"  # DLQ\n    enriched_events_topic: str = \"enriched_events\"\n\nclass IngestionServiceSettings(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Configuration specific to Ingestion Service\"\"\"\n    service_name: str = \"ingestion-service\"\n\n    # Batch source configuration\n    batch_sources: List[BatchSourceConfig]  # S3 URIs, schedules\n\n    # Stream source configuration\n    stream_sources: List[StreamSourceConfig]  # External Kafka topics\n\n    # Output topic (where to publish raw events)\n    output_topic: str = \"raw_events\"\n\n    consumer_group: str = \"ingestion-service-cg\"\n\nclass ValidationServiceSettings(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Configuration specific to Validation Service\"\"\"\n    service_name: str = \"validation-service\"\n\n    # Input/output topics\n    input_topic: str = \"raw_events\"\n    valid_output_topic: str = \"valid_events\"\n    invalid_output_topic: str = \"invalid_events\"\n\n    # Validation strategies to apply\n    strategies: List[str] = [\"schema\", \"great_expectations\", \"toxicity_bounds\"]\n\n    # GE configuration\n    expectation_suite_name: str = \"pulstream_core_stream_events\"\n\n    consumer_group: str = \"validation-service-cg\"\n\nclass ProcessingServiceSettings(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Configuration specific to Processing Service\"\"\"\n    service_name: str = \"processing-service\"\n\n    # Input/output topics\n    input_topic: str = \"valid_events\"\n    output_topic: str = \"enriched_events\"\n\n    # Transformation strategies to apply\n    transformers: List[str] = [\"sentiment\", \"toxicity\", \"virality\"]\n\n    # Concurrency settings\n    max_concurrency: int = 64\n\n    consumer_group: str = \"processing-service-cg\"\n\nclass SinkServiceSettings(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Configuration specific to Sink/Persistence Service\"\"\"\n    service_name: str = \"sink-service\"\n\n    input_topic: str = \"enriched_events\"\n\n    # Data Lake configuration\n    data_lake_path: str = \"s3://pulstream/lake/\"\n    data_lake_format: str = \"delta\"\n\n    consumer_group: str = \"sink-service-cg\"\n\n# Root configuration now composes service configs\nclass PulseStreamNexusSettings(BaseSettings):\n    \"\"\"Root configuration for the entire platform\"\"\"\n    env: str = \"development\"\n\n    # Shared infrastructure\n    message_broker: MessageBrokerSettings\n    service_discovery: ServiceDiscoverySettings\n    monitoring: MonitoringSettings\n\n    # Per-service configurations (deployed independently)\n    ingestion: IngestionServiceSettings\n    validation: ValidationServiceSettings\n    processing: ProcessingServiceSettings\n    sink: SinkServiceSettings\n```\n\n#### Key Configuration Shifts:\n\n1. **From Single Pipeline to Service Mesh**: Configuration moves from defining a single linear pipeline to defining independent services with their own lifecycles.\n\n2. **Topic-Based Contracts**: Services communicate through well-defined Kafka topics. The topic names become the API contract between services.\n\n3. **Independent Consumer Groups**: Each service has its own consumer group, allowing independent scaling and replay.\n\n4. **Strategy Configuration Per Service**: Each service declares which strategies/transformers it will apply, loaded from configuration rather than hard-coded.\n\n5. **Source-Agnostic Processing**: The validation and processing services don't know whether data came from batch (S3) or real-time (Kafka) sources - they just consume from the `raw_events` topic.\n\n## Summary\n\n- **Primary finding**: `module_30.py` (BackfillRunner) is the monolithic bottleneck component that tightly couples ingestion, validation, and transformation, making it unsuitable for real-time integration.\n\n- **Key components**:\n  - `/app/project/src/module_30.py` - Monolithic batch orchestrator (bottleneck)\n  - `/app/project/src/module_17.py` - Streaming processor with reusable validation/transformation logic\n  - `/app/project/src/module_8.py` - Processing pipeline with Strategy and Observer patterns\n  - `/app/project/src/module_37.py` - Data quality validation strategies\n  - `/app/project/src/config.py` - Configuration that needs restructuring for microservices\n\n- **Architectural pattern**: **Event-Driven Architecture (EDA)** with Kafka as the message bus, decomposing the monolith into:\n  1. **Ingestion Service** - Handles both batch (S3) and real-time (Kafka) sources\n  2. **Validation Service** - Applies schema and domain validation\n  3. **Processing Service** - Applies enrichment transformations\n  4. **Sink Service** - Persists to data lake/warehouse\n\n- **Recommendations**:\n  1. Refactor `module_30.py` to extract ingestion logic into a dedicated Ingestion Service\n  2. Create a Validation Service reusing `DataValidator` from `module_17.py` and `DataQualityMonitor` from `module_37.py`\n  3. Create a Processing Service reusing `ProcessingPipeline` and `TransformStrategy` implementations from `module_8.py`\n  4. Restructure `config.py` to define service-specific configurations and topic-based contracts\n  5. Introduce a canonical event format that both batch and real-time sources normalize to\n  6. Implement dead-letter queues for failed validation/processing for observability\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
